Let's derive efficient objective and gradient using FFT to accelerate smooth max optimization. We can compute all c_m via convolution/correlation. But we need gradient of smooth max. If we use logsumexp over all lags, weights w_k. Then gradient = sum w_k dc_k/dh. We can compute all lag gradients via convolution of w with h as described. We can compute using FFT for arbitrary weights faster O(n log n). Let's formulate arrays p_m for positive lag (k=m) and q_m for negative lag (k=-m). Let w_pos[m] = weight of c_m (m=0..n-1); w_neg[m] = weight of c_{-m} (m=1..n-1). For m=0, c_0=∑ h_i(1-h_i); gradient =1-2h.

For m>=1:
Positive: ∑_{i=0}^{n-m-1} (h_i(1-h_{i+m})). Negative: ∑_{i=0}^{n-m-1} (h_{i+m}(1-h_i)). (Based on correlate orientation). So gradient contribution:
Let L=n-m.
For indices i=0..L-1 (first positions):
their appearance as first in pos: w_pos*(1-h[i+m])
as second in neg: w_neg*(-h[i+m])? Negative term h_{i+m}(1-h_i), derivative wrt h_i (first of negative pair) = - h_{i+m}. Yes.
So g[0:L] += w_pos[m]*(1 - h[m:m+L]) - w_neg[m]*h[m:m+L].
For indices i=m..n-1 (second positions):
pos derivative wrt h_{i+m} = -h[i]
neg derivative wrt h_{i+m} = 1 - h[i]
So g[m:n] += -w_pos[m]*h[0:L] + w_neg[m]*(1 - h[0:L]).
If w_pos=w_neg=w:
g[0:L]+=w*(1-2h[m:]); g[m:n]+=w*(1-2h[0:]). Great.

Let's design a robust gradient optimizer:

Function `all_lags(h,n,dx)`: c = np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full')*dx.
Function `top_grad(h,n,c,alpha,topM)`: returns gradient using weights of topM lags (not all). Actually we can choose topM lags by c and equal weight? A common trick: minimize sum of top M lags (soft). Use weights = softmax over topM? Simple gradient = average gradient of top M lags. The max tag mostly active. To be differentiable, use log-sum-exp over top M with large alpha. We can compute weights for top M. For each selected lag, we can compute its exact gradient in O(n) using vector operations; total O(M n). M maybe 8-32. This is efficient. We don't need weights for all.

Lag gradient exact for one positive/negative:
For positive lag m:
g[0:n-m] += 1 - h[m:n]
g[m:n] += -h[0:n-m]
For negative m:
g[0:n-m] += -h[m:n]
g[m:n] += 1 - h[0:n-m]
For lag0: g += 1 -2h.
Using exact top lags with softmax weight. This is easy and O(M n). We'll select top M lags from c by value. Need include lag0. If multiple near max, softmax weights.

This gradient approximates direction reducing top lags. Projected gradient with line search/backtracking and momentum? Since nonconvex, we can do:
- Compute c, c5.
- Compute g_smooth for top M weights.
- Normalize gradient? Step size maybe based on c/n. Use backtracking: try step*factor until c5(h_new) decreases (or for first few allow increase to escape plateau?). Since objective nonconvex, plain descent may get stuck. We can add perturbations.
- Use Adam? Adaptive moments help. But projection with Adam on box/sum? We can implement projected Adam: maintain m,v, update x = h - lr*m/√(v+eps), project. This can handle non-flat. Need line search? Adam without line search risky. Use small learning rate and momentum. Previous used momentum. We can incorporate backtracking after projected step: evaluate c_new; if not decreasing for many, reduce lr.
- Also use Frank-Wolfe? likely not.

Potential issue: sum constraint; projection via bisection.

Let's implement projection onto {0≤h≤1, sum=target}. The Euclidean projection formula:
Given x, find λ such that sum(clip(x-λ,0,1))=target. Since f(λ) decreasing continuous. Lower bound = max(0,target? Actually if target between0,n). We can bisect λ between min(x)-1 and max(x)+1. Or use simpler scaling? Euclidean better. 60 iterations O(n) okay. For speed, 30 iterations.

Function `project_sum_box(x,n)`:
target=n/2.
lo = min(x)-1.0
hi = max(x)+1.0
for 50: mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.clip(x-mid,0,1).sum(); if s>target: lo=mid else hi=mid. After, return clip(x-mid,0,1). Then final exact adjust residual: diff = target - sum; if diff>0 add to entries <1 proportional room maybe; else subtract from >0; finally clip; if residual tiny adjust one entry. The bisection sum may be off <1e-12, final distribute.

Need maintain sum exactly. Use final `enforce_sum`.

Detailed gradient descent routine:
Input h (feasible), n, deadline, top_m, alpha0, min_time.
- best_h,best_c.
- For each "run" (restart) with small allocation:
  - h = h0 + noise (if not first) projected.
  - Reset descent_state (maybe v=0).
  - current_c = c5.
  - step_size initial = 0.2? direction unit.
  - for iter until time:
    c = compute_c_array; current c5.
    top_idx = top_m indices (argpartition). weights for smooth: if top_m >1 maybe softmax alpha=1000. Or use average top M? For gradient use softmax weights to emphasize max.
    grad = sum weights lag_grad * dx.
    norm = l2(grad); if norm<1e-12: break.
    direction = -grad/norm
    # momentum direction: if have mom_dir, dir = (1-beta)*direction + beta*mom_dir, renormalize.
    # try step schedule:
    improved=False
    for factor in [step_base, step_base*0.5,... down to 1e-4]:
       h_try=project(h + factor*dir, n)
       c_try = c5(c_try)
       if c_try < current_c - 1e-13:
           h=h_try; current_c=c_try; improved=True
           step_base = min(max_step, factor*1.05? maybe increase if successful small? keep)
           break
       elif c_try < current_c - 1e-15: ...
    if not improved:
       # maybe accept small increase to escape? use best? No.
       patience++ ; reduce step; if patience>... seed noise and reset.
    if current_c < best_c: update best.
- Return best.

Let's implement a helper `pair_pos_delta(h, src, dst, delta, m)`:
For positive lag m between 0 and n-1-m pairs low a, high b=a+m.
 delta contribution from h_a change (δa) and h_b change (δb). Only if a==src -> δa=-d; a==dst -> +d; b==src -> -d; b==dst -> +d.
 We can compute by checking if pair includes src/dst. Since only four pairs per m, but loop m.
Pseudo:
```
def transfer_c5_after(c, h, src, dst, d, n):
    c_new = c.copy()
    # m=0: c0 changes: sum h(1-h). Pair (i,i). src/dst changes: f=t(1-t), derivative. Δ = new-old for src and dst.
    for t in (src,dst) unique:
        old=h[t]; if t==src: new=old-d; else new=old+d
        c0_change += new*(1-new)-old*(1-old)
    c_new[n-1]+=c0_change*dx? c already includes dx? store scaled => c0_change*dx.
    for m in range(1,n):
       # pos
       idxp = n-1+m; idxn = n-1-m
       Δp=0
       for a in range(0,n-m): maybe too much? We can't loop pairs; need only if a/src/dst or a+m/src/dst.
```
Better loop m and check four cases:
Positive:
- If src >=0 and src+m<n: a=src, b=src+m. δa=-d; δb = d if b==dst else 0. Δ = δa*(1-h_b) - h_a*δb - δa*δb.
- If dst+m<n and dst != src and dst+m != src? a=dst, b=dst+m. δa=+d; δb = -d if b==src else 0. (if b==src pair included? Need avoid double if same pair counted in src first? In src first if src+m=dst, counted. In dst first if dst+m=src only if src>dst+m, a different pair; no double unless impossible same? If src<dst, pair src,dst counted in src first. Pair dst,src (low dst high src) counted in dst first. Distinct. If src>dst, pair src,dst counted in dst first? Let's use conditions b==dst add. Also if dst+m=src, that's pair low dst high src, distinct from high? At most one pair connects src/dst for given orientation? Actually for m=|src-dst|, one pair includes both if low=min(src,dst), high=max. Our cases: if min is src (src<dst): src first b=dst; if min is dst (dst<src): dst first b=src. If we loop both src-first and dst-first, we count one. Need ensure not double: if src+m<n and (dst? b=dst) okay; if dst+m<n and b=src only when src>dst, src-first false because src+m>dst? if src>dst, src+m< n but b=src+m not dst; okay no double. If src<dst, dst+m could equal? dst+m<src impossible positive. So fine.)

Let's design `optimize_with_pair_search(h,n,deadline, rng, max_rounds=None)`:
- Feasible h; c = c_array; c5 = c.max(); best.
- rounds=0; stagnation=0
- while time < deadline - 1:
   rounds +=1
   improved = local_step(h,c,c5, rounds)
   if improved: stagnation=0; best update if c5<best; else maybe accept?
   else:
      stagnation +=1
      if stagnation > 20:
         # try a random perturb from best
         if time < deadline-5:
             h = project(best + rng.normal(0,0.02*stagnation/20?, n))
             c=c_array; c5=c.max(); stagnation=0
             if c5>=best: maybe continue from best
         else break
      if stagnation > 200: break
- return best_h,best_c

```
def generate_candidates(h,c,n,M=R?):
   top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -M)[-M:]
   cands=[]

      lag=idx-(n-1)
      if lag==0: ...

         m=abs(lag); L=n-m
         if lag>0:
             vals = h[:L]*(1-h[m:n])
             # only if h[:L] > h[m:n]? Actually high->low. Also low->high candidates maybe.

             # For each i: if h[i] > h[i+m]: src=i,dst=i+m; other src=i+m,dst=i (opposite).

             vals = h[m:n]*(1-h[:L]) # high*(1-low)
             for i: high=i+m, low=i; if h[high]>h[low]: src=high,dst=low; opposite too.
```

```
def pair_search(h, n, deadline, rng, use_anneal=True, max_no_improve=60):
    h = h.copy(); c=compute_c_array(h,n); c5=c.max(); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5
    T=1e-5; stagn=0; no_improve=0
    while time<deadline-2:
       cands=generate...
       accepted=False
       for cand in cands:
           newc,new5=fast_move...
           if new5 < c5 - 1e-14:
               accept; c5=new5; no_improve=0; accepted=True; break
           elif use_anneal and new5 < c5 + T and rng.random() < exp(-(new5-c5)/T):
               accept; no_improve+=1? accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > max_no_improve:
              # perturb from best: h=project(best + noise); c=...; c5=c.max(); no_improve=0; T*=0.5
              if time<deadline-5: continue
              else break
       if c5 < best_c: update
       T *= 0.999? decay
       if no_improve > 200: break
       # occasional recompute c
    return best
```

```
def enforce_sum(h,n):
  target=n/2.0
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>0:
     if diff>0:
        room=np.where(h<1, 1-h,0); order? Add small to any with room.
        if room.sum()>abs(diff): h += diff*room/room.sum() (may break sum due rounding)
        else add to first.
     else: h += diff * ... (subtract proportional from positive)

  diff=target-h.sum()

     for i in range(n): if h[i]<1:
         add=min(diff,1-h[i]); h[i]+=add; diff-=add; if diff==0: break

  # correct possible >1 due float: clip and adjust again?
```

```
def fast_move_max(c, h, src, dst, d, n, dx):
    c_new = c.copy()
    # c0
    os = h[src]; ns = os - d; c_new[n-1] += (ns*(1-ns) - os*(1-os))*dx
    od = h[dst]; nd = od + d; c_new[n-1] += (nd*(1-nd) - od*(1-od))*dx
    for m in range(1,n):

        # event 1: src first
        a=src; b=src+m
        if b < n:
            da=-d; db = d if b==dst else 0.0
            ha=h[a]; hb=h[b]
            dp += da*(1-hb) - ha*db - da*db
            dn += db*(1-ha) - hb*da - db*da

        else: lp=0
        # event 2: src second
        a=src-m
        if a >=0:
            if lp and a==src? no duplicate? Need duplicate with event 4 if dst? We'll track last pair.
```

Detailed pair_search pseudocode (top-level):
```
def pair_search(h, n, deadline, rng, max_lags=8, max_pairs=4, max_no_improve=50, temp0=1e-5):
    h = np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c = compute_c_array(h,n)
    c5 = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = c5
    temp = temp0
    no_improve = 0
    accepts = 0
    rounds = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
       rounds +=1
       cands = generate_candidates(h,c,n,max_lags,max_pairs)
       accepted = False
       if len(cands)==0:
           # random candidates
           for _ in range(20): ...
       else:
           for src,dst,d in cands:
               if d <= 1e-12 or d > h[src]+1e-12 or d > 1-h[dst]+1e-12: continue
               # if h[src]-d < -1e-13 etc skip
               newc,new5 = fast_move_max(c,h,src,dst,d,n)
               if new5 < c5 - 1e-14:
                   accept; break
               # anneal if no greedy? We can try anneal but accept worse maybe. Use only after no greedy in candidates? For each candidate, if new5 < c5 and rng < exp? Actually if new5 not better, could accept random. We can after finishing greedy candidates and none accepted, do a separate random perturbation.
       if accepted: ...
       else:
          no_improve +=1

             # attempt one random move (from cands or random) with annealing
             ...

             h2 = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n), n)
             c = compute_c_array(h2,n); c5=float(c.max())
             # If h2 worse, maybe still use to escape, but if too bad, set h=best
             no_improve=0; accepts=0; temp*=0.5
             if time.time() > deadline - 2: break
             continue
          if no_improve > max_no_improve*3: break
       if c5 < best_c - 1e-15:
           best_c=c5; best_h=h.copy()
       if accepts % 100 == 0:
           c=compute_c_array(h,n); c5=float(c.max())
           if c5 < best_c: update
       temp *= 0.995
    # finalize: project, recompute
    h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    c=compute_c_array(h,n); c5=c.max()
    return h,c5
```

Pseudo optimized:
```
def fast_move_max(c,h,src,dst,dd,n):
    dx = 2.0/n
    cn = c.copy()
    idx0 = n-1
    os=h[src]; ns=os-dd; cn[idx0] += (ns*(1-ns)-os*(1-os))*dx
    od=h[dst]; nd=od+dd; cn[idx0] += (nd*(1-nd)-od*(1-od))*dx
    absd = src-dst
    if absd <0: absd=-absd
    skip_src_second = (src > dst and m_abs? no per m)
    for m in range(1,n):
       dp=0.0; dn=0.0
       # src first
       b=src+m
       if b<n:
          da=-dd; db = dd if b==dst else 0.0
          hb=h[b]; ha=os? src h. Use h[src] old; but h not modified. For speed h_src = old src, h_dst old dst.
          dp += da*(1-hb) - hs*db - da*db
          dn += db*(1-hs) - hb*da - db*da
       # dst second duplicate if src<dst and m==absd
       if src > dst and m==absd:
           # duplicate pair low dst high src appears in dst first (not yet) and src second; skip src second
           pass
       else:
          a=src-m
          if a>=0:
             da = dd if a==dst else 0.0
             db = -dd
             ha=h[a]; hb=hs
             dp += da*(1-hb) - ha*db - da*db
             dn += db*(1-ha) - hb*da - db*da
       # dst first duplicate if src>dst and m==absd? skip? We chose skip src second; so not duplicate.
       b=dst+m
       if b<n:
          da=dd; db = -dd if b==src else 0.0
          hb=h[b]; hd=old dst
          dp += da*(1-hb) - hd*db - da*db
          dn += db*(1-hd) - hb*da - db*da
       # dst second duplicate if src<dst and m==absd skip
       if src < dst and m==absd:
           pass
       else:
          a=dst-m
          if a>=0:
             da = -dd if a==src else 0.0
             db = dd
             ha=h[a]; hb=hd
             dp += da*(1-hb) - ha*db - da*db
             dn += db*(1-ha) - hb*da - db*da
       if dp: cn[idx0+m] += dp*dx
       if dn: cn[idx0-m] += dn*dx
    return cn, float(cn.max())
```
But in src first, if b==dst, db=dd, formula includes -hs*db - da*db = -hs*dd + dd^2. Good.
In dst first, if b==src, db=-dd, formula da=dd, hb=h_src. product old h_dst(1-hsrc), new hdst+dd, hsrc-dd. Δ=dd(1-hsrc) - hdst*(-dd) - dd*(-dd)? =dd(1-hsrc)+hdst dd+dd^2. Formula da*(1-hb)-ha*db-da*db = dd(1-hs)-hd*(-dd)-dd*(-dd)=dd(1-hs)+hd dd+dd^2. ok.
In src second (pair a=src-m,b=src), db=-dd. If a==dst, da=dd. Formula dp: da(1-hs)-ha*(-dd)-da*(-dd)=dd(1-hs)+ha dd+dd^2. Pair low dst high src for positive: old hd(1-hs), new hd+dd, hs-dd => same. Good.
In dst second (pair a=dst-m,b=dst), db=dd. If a==src da=-dd. dp formula: -dd(1-hd) - hs*dd -(-dd*dd)= -dd(1-hd)-hs dd+dd^2. Pair low src high dst for positive: old hs(1-hd), new hs-dd, hd+dd => -dd(1-hd)-hs dd+dd^2. good.
Check duplicate skip: Case src<dst, m=absd: src first pair(src,dst); dst second pair(src,dst) duplicate; skip dst second. good.
Case src>dst, m=absd: dst first pair(dst,src); src second pair(dst,src) duplicate; skip src second. good.

```
def add_candidate(cands, seen, h, src, dst):
    dmax = min(h[src], 1.0 - h[dst])
    if dmax <= 1e-10: return
    fracs=(0.9,0.6,0.3,1.0)
    for fr in fracs:
       d=fr*dmax
       if d <=1e-10: continue
       key=(src,dst,int(round(d, 9)))
       if key not in seen:
          seen.add(key); cands.append((src,dst,d))
```

```
def random_candidates(h,n,rng,count, max_cands):
  cands=[]; seen=set()
  for _ in range(count):
    src=int(rng.randint(0,n)); dst=int(rng.randint(0,n))
    if src==dst: continue
    # choose direction: if h[src] > h[dst]: reduce pair to high/low? random dir

       s=src; d=dst
```

Now, gradient_search implementation details:
```
def gradient_search(h,n,deadline,rng,top_m=8,alpha=300):
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n); c=compute_c_array; c5; best
    step=0.3; stagn=0; iter=0
    while time<deadline-1:
      c=compute_c_array(h,n); c5=float(c.max())
      if c5<best: update
      top_idx=np.argsort(c)[-top_m:][::-1]
      vals=c[top_idx]
      # softmax weights
      m=vals.max(); sh=alpha*(vals-m); mx=sh.max(); w=np.exp(sh-mx); w/=w.sum()
      g=np.zeros(n)
      dx=2/n
      for wk, idx in zip(w, top_idx):
         if wk<1e-10: continue
         lag=idx-(n-1)
         if lag==0: ...
      norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if norm < 1e-12: break
      dir = -g/norm
      accepted=False
      for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
         h_try=project_feasible(h + step*mult*dir,n)
         c_try=compute_c_array; c_try5=...
         if c_try5 < c5 - 1e-13:
             h=h_try; c=c_try; c5=c_try5; accepted=True; break
      if accepted:
         stagn=0; step=min(1.0, step*1.05)
      else:
         stagn +=1; step *= 0.8
         if stagn > 30:
             # perturb best
             h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0,0.02,n), n)
             c=...; c5=...; step=0.2; stagn=0
         if step < 1e-4: break
      if c5 < best: update
    return best_h,best_c
```
Use `zip(w, top_idx)` where top_idx array; okay no lambda.

```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,deadline, max_rounds=2, alpha=500, top_m=10):
  best_h=h; best_c=compute_c5
  bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
  cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_eq_sum_fun,'jac':_eq_sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
  hh=project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
  for r in range(max_rounds):
    if time... break
    c=compute_c_array(hh,n)
    if top_m>=c.size: top=np.arange(c.size) else top=np.argsort(c)[-top_m:]
    try:
      res=minimize(_obj_fixed_top, hh, args=(n, top, alpha), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':50,'ftol':1e-12})
      hh2=project_feasible(res.x,n)
      c2=compute_c_array(hh2,n); c52=...
      if c52 < best_c: best=hh2
      hh=hh2
    except: pass
  return best,best_c
```
Need `bounds` list length n. For n=1000, memory ok but SLSQP may be too slow. Only call if n<=700 and time>60. `maxiter` 30.

Project feasible bisection details:
```
def project_feasible(x, n):
    x = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    target = 0.5 * n
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    # bisection
    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = np.sum(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x - mid,0,1)
    # adjust residual exactly
    diff = target - float(np.sum(y))
    if diff > 0:
       # add to entries with room, starting largest room? Use proportional to avoid many
       room = 1.0 - y
       tot = room.sum()
       if tot > 0:
           y += diff * room / tot
           y = np.clip(y,0,1)
           diff = target - float(np.sum(y))
    elif diff < 0:
       tot = y.sum()
       if tot > 0:
           y += diff * y / tot
           y = np.clip(y,0,1)
           diff = target - float(np.sum(y))
    if diff > 1e-14:
       # add one by one
       for i in range(n):
           if diff <= 0: break
           add = min(diff, 1.0 - y[i])
           if add > 0:
               y[i] += add; diff -= add
    elif diff < -1e-14:
       for i in range(n):
           if diff >= 0: break
           sub = min(-diff, y[i])
           if sub > 0:
               y[i] -= sub; diff += sub
    return y
```
If proportional step then clip can reintroduce residual. Final loop ensures exact within maybe 1e-15. But adding `add` can exceed 1 by tiny? use min. Sum may still off by 1e-16; okay. For exact target integer, if diff tiny and all room? We can after loop if abs(diff)>0 and some entry has room adjust. But modulo float.
Need not bisection if x already feasible? But okay. 60*O(n) per projection. Projections in gradient many; 60*500=30k per proj, if 1000 projections 30M, okay. Could reduce to 35 iterations. Use 40.

In pair_search, no projection after moves except perturb; so fast.

`compute_c_array` returns new array; use `np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * (2.0/n)`. For n large, direct. Could use `np.float64`.

`smooth_top_weights`:
```
def smooth_top_weights(vals, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    sh = alpha * (vals - m)
    mx = float(np.max(sh))
    if mx > 500:
       w=np.exp(sh-mx)
    else:
       w=np.exp(sh)
    s=w.sum()
    if s==0: w = np.ones_like(vals)/len(vals)
    else: w/=s
    return w
```
vals length small; no need.

`top_gradient(h,n,c,top_idx,alpha)` where top_idx maybe array. Compute vals=c[top_idx], weights. Then loop. Use `dx=2/n`. For each idx in top_idx:
```
w = weights[j]
if w<1e-12: continue
lag = idx - (n-1)
if lag ==0: g += w*(1-2h)
else:
  m = lag if lag>0 else -lag
  L=n-m
  if lag>0:
     g[:L] += w*(1-h[m:])
     g[m:] += -w*h[:L]
  else:
     g[:L] += -w*h[m:]
     g[m:] += w*(1-h[:L])
return g*dx
```
Actually for negative lag formula earlier: c_neg=∑ h_{i+m}(1-h_i). Gradient: low i (first index in sum) derivative -h[i+m]; high i+m derivative 1-h[i]. So g[:L] += -w*h[m:]; g[m:] += w*(1-h[:L]). Good.

SLSQP fixed top objective: uses logsum over fixed top. Need pass `top_idx` to args; but objective calls np.correlate each time. If top_idx includes all length. We can implement `_obj_fixed_top(x,n,top_idx,alpha)` and `_jac_fixed_top`.
In `_jac_fixed_top`, compute c and vals; weights; then loop; return grad*dx. Use same top_gradient? That function computes weights and needs c. Could call `top_gradient(x,n,c,top_idx,alpha)`. But top_gradient also computes weights; okay. It expects c array. For objective, need logsum. Separate.

```
def logsumexp_values(vals, alpha):
   m=vals.max(); sh=alpha*(vals-m); mx=sh.max(); if mx>500: return m + (mx+log(sum(exp(sh-mx))))/alpha else m+log(sum(exp(sh)))/alpha
```

```
def run(...):

    n_main = len(h_init)
    h_main = project_feasible(h_init,n_main)
    c5=compute_c5
 else:
    n_main=500; h_main=...; c5=...
 best_h=h_main; best_c=c5
 # Main pair search
 if deadline - t0 > 60:
    alloc = min(450, (deadline-t0)*0.45)
    hp,cp=pair_search(h_main,n_main,t0+alloc,rng)
    update
 # Gradient search from best (if time)
 if deadline - t0 > 80:
    alloc = min(120, (deadline-t0)*0.25)
    hp,cp=gradient_search(best_h,len(best_h),t0+alloc,rng)
    update
 # SLSQP polish if n<=600 and time
 n_cur=len(best_h)
 if n_cur <= 620 and deadline - t0 > 70:
    alloc = min(70, (deadline-t0)*0.3)
    hp,cp=slsqp_polish(best_h,n_cur,t0+alloc)
    update
 # Try one higher resolution
 # maybe n_high = max(n_cur+150, 650) even. If time >120
 n_candidates=[]
 for ntry in [n_cur+128, 700, 900]: if ntry> n_cur? even; break.
 for ntry in candidates:
    if deadline-t0 < 90: break
    alloc=min(100, (deadline-t0)*0.25)
    h_try=interpolate best to ntry; project
    # maybe if try c lower? pair search
    hp,cp=pair_search(h_try,ntry,t0+alloc,rng,max_lags=6,max_pairs=3)
    update
 # Final pair search with remaining
 if best_h is not None and deadline - t0 > 20:
    hp,cp=pair_search(best_h,len(best_h),deadline-1,rng,max_lags=10,max_pairs=4)
    update
 # final feasibility/recompute
 h=project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h)); c=compute_c_array; c5
 return h, float(c5), len(h)
```

`binarize`: choose top n//2 to 1, rest 0. If n odd, target n/2 .5 not integer; select k=floor(n/2) ones and one 0.5? Simpler for even. We use even.
```
def binarize(h,n):
  k=n//2
  idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
  out=np.zeros(n); out[idx[:k]]=1.0
  # if n odd, set one 0.5? maybe
  if n %2 ==1: out[idx[k]]=0.5
  return out
```
Need sum exactly n/2: even ok; odd with one .5. Good.

```
def thue_morse_start(n, rng):
  out=np.empty(n)

  # adjust sum to n/2: if sum not target, flip bits
  target=n/2.0; s=out.sum()
  if s > target:
     idx=np.where(out==1)[0]
     # flip smallest? order random
  ...
```

Let's write Fast_move carefully with no mistakes.

```
def fast_move_max(c, h, src, dst, d, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    cn = c.copy()
    idx0 = n - 1
    hs = h[src]; hd = h[dst]
    ns = hs - d; cn[idx0] += (ns*(1.0-ns) - hs*(1.0-hs))*dx
    nd = hd + d; cn[idx0] += (nd*(1.0-nd) - hd*(1.0-hd))*dx
    absd = src - dst
    if absd < 0: absd = -absd
    # local variables for speed
    nn = n; oo = idx0
    for m in range(1, nn):
        dp = 0.0; dn = 0.0
        # src first
        b = src + m
        if b < nn:
            da = -d
            db = d if (b == dst) else 0.0
            hb = h[b]
            # ha = hs
            dp += da * (1.0 - hb) - hs * db - da * db
            dn += db * (1.0 - hs) - hb * da - db * da
        # src second
        use = True
        if src > dst and m == absd:
            use = False
        if use:
            a = src - m
            if a >= 0:
                da = d if (a == dst) else 0.0
                db = -d
                ha = h[a]
                # hb = hs
                dp += da * (1.0 - hs) - ha * db - da * db
                dn += db * (1.0 - ha) - hs * da - db * da
        # dst first
        b = dst + m
        if b < nn:
            da = d
            db = -d if (b == src) else 0.0
            hb = h[b]
            # ha = hd
            dp += da * (1.0 - hb) - hd * db - da * db
            dn += db * (1.0 - hd) - hb * da - db * da
        # dst second
        use = True
        if src < dst and m == absd:
            use = False
        if use:
            a = dst - m
            if a >= 0:
                da = -d if (a == src) else 0.0
                db = d
                ha = h[a]
                # hb = hd
                dp += da * (1.0 - hd) - ha * db - da * db
                dn += db * (1.0 - ha) - hd * da - db * da
        if dp != 0.0:
            cn[oo + m] += dp * dx
        if dn != 0.0:
            cn[oo - m] += dn * dx
    return cn, float(cn.max())
```
Check dn in src second: pair a=src-m, b=src. term high=b, low=a. db=-d, da=d if a==dst. Δn = db*(1-ha)-hb*da-db*da = -d*(1-ha)-hs*da -(-d*da) = -d(1-ha)-hs da+ d da. If a==dst, da=d: -d(1-hd)-hs d + d^2, matches formula? Pair low dst high src, terms: old hs(1-hd), new hs-d, hd+d => -d(1-hd)-hs d + d^2. yes.
For dst second: pair a=dst-m,b=dst. db=d, da=-d if a=src. Δn=d*(1-ha)-hd*da-d*da =d(1-ha)+hd d + d^2 if a=src. Pair low src high dst: old hd(1-hs), new hd+d, hs-d => d(1-hs)+hd d+d^2. yes.

c0 change if src==dst? never. If d = 0 skip.

Potential issue: If source index > destination and m=absd skip src second; but what about dst first in same m: pair low dst high src, included. Good. If src<dst skip dst second; src first included. Good.

Now, candidate generation:
```
def add_candidate(cands, seen, h, src, dst, max_cands):
    if len(cands) >= max_cands: return
    if src == dst: return
    lim = min(h[src], 1.0 - h[dst])
    if lim <= 1e-11: return
    lim = max(0.0, lim - 1e-12)
    fracs = (0.9, 0.6, 0.3, 1.0)
    for fr in fracs:
       d=fr*lim
       if d <= 1e-10: continue
       key = (src, dst, int(round(d*1000000.0)))
       if key in seen: continue
       seen.add(key); cands.append((src,dst,d))
       if len(cands) >= max_cands: return
```
Using 1e6 tolerance may miss very small differences but okay. If d extremely small, rounding key duplicate; skip. Maybe use 1e7.

`generate_candidates`:
```
def generate_candidates(h,c,n,max_lags,max_pairs,max_cands=350):
    cands=[]; seen=set()
    size=2*n-1
    if max_lags >= size:
       top_idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1]
    else:
       top_idx=np.argsort(c)[-max_lags:][::-1]
    for idx in top_idx:
       lag=idx-(n-1)
       if lag == 0:
          hi_count = min(4, n//2)
          if hi_count>0:
             hi=np.argsort(h)[-hi_count:][::-1]
             lo_count=hi_count
             lo=np.argsort(h)[:lo_count]
             for li in lo:
               if h[li] >= 0.5: continue
               for hi_i in hi:
                 if h[hi_i] <= 0.5: continue
                 add_candidate(cands,seen,h,int(li),int(hi_i),max_cands)
                 if len(cands)>=max_cands: break
               if len(cands)>=max_cands: break
       else:
          m= lag if lag>0 else -lag
          L=n-m
          if lag>0:
             vals=h[:L]*(1.0-h[m:n])
          else:
             vals=h[m:n]*(1.0-h[:L])
          if vals.size ==0: continue
          k=max_pairs
          if k >= vals.size:
             pp=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
          else:
             pp=np.argsort(vals)[-k:][::-1]
          for p in pp:
             p=int(p)
             if lag>0:
                add_candidate(cands,seen,h,p,p+m,max_cands)
             else:
                add_candidate(cands,seen,h,p+m,p,max_cands)
             # opposite if budget
             if len(cands) < max_cands//2:
                if lag>0:
                   add_candidate(cands,seen,h,p+m,p,max_cands)
                else:
                   add_candidate(cands,seen,h,p,p+m,max_cands)
             if len(cands)>=max_cands: break
       if len(cands)>=max_cands: break
    return cands
```
For lag>0 opposite p+m,p may be same as primary? no unless m=0. Good.
`max_cands//2` threshold: only add opposite until half filled. Maybe not; add for top pairs only. Good.

`random_candidates` top-level:
```
def random_candidates(h,n,rng,count,max_cands=100):
   cands=[]; seen=set()
   for _ in range(count):
      if len(cands)>=max_cands: break
      src=int(rng.randint(0,n)); dst=int(rng.randint(0,n))
      if src==dst: continue
      # choose direction randomly, but bias to reduce c0: if h[src]>h[dst] maybe src high? random
      # We'll add both directions for some
      if rng.random() < 0.5:
         add_candidate(cands,seen,h,src,dst,max_cands)
      else:
         add_candidate(cands,seen,h,dst,src,max_cands)
   return cands
```
If all 0.5, both directions and d=0.5 creates moves.

```
def pair_search(h,n,deadline,rng,max_lags=8,max_pairs=4,max_cands=350,max_no_improve=60,temp0? no):

    no_improve=0; accepts=0; perturb_count=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
       cands=generate_candidates(...)
       accepted=False
       if len(cands)==0:
          cands=random_candidates(...)
       # also if no_improve>10, include random
       if no_improve > 10:
          rc=random_candidates(...)
          # append up to limit? maybe use concat
```

```
       for src,dst,d in cands:
           if time.time() > deadline-1: break
```

Now, gradient_search code. Need not too long.
```
def top_gradients(h,n,c,top_idx,alpha):
   vals=c[top_idx]; weights=...
   g=np.zeros(n)
   dx=2.0/n
   for j in range(len(top_idx)):
      idx=top_idx[j]
      w=weights[j]
      if w < 1e-12: continue
      lag = idx - (n-1)
      if lag ==0: g += w*(1.0-2.0*h)
      else:
         m=lag if lag>0 else -lag; L=n-m
         if lag>0:
            g[:L] += w*(1.0-h[m:])
            g[m:] += -w*h[:L]
         else:
            g[:L] += -w*h[m:]
            g[m:] += w*(1.0-h[:L])
   return g*dx
```
Use `len(top_idx)` not zip maybe. If top_idx is np array, len okay.

`gradient_search`:
```
def gradient_search(h,n,deadline,rng,top_m=8,alpha=300):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c_array; c5=...
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5
    step=0.3; stagn=0
    while time < deadline-1:
       c=compute_c_array(h,n); c5=float(c.max())
       if c5 < best_c: update
       top_idx=np.argsort(c)[-top_m:][::-1]
       g=top_gradients(h,n,c,top_idx,alpha)
       norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if norm < 1e-12: break
       direction=-g/norm
       accepted=False
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125):
           h_try=project_feasible(h + step*mult*direction, n)
           c_try=compute_c_array(h_try,n); c5_try=float(c_try.max())
           if c5_try < c5 - 1e-13:
              h=h_try; c=c_try; c5=c5_try; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           stagn=0
           step = min(1.0, step*1.1)
       else:
           stagn +=1
           step *= 0.8
           if stagn > 40:
               h=project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0,0.02,n),n)
               c=compute_c_array; c5=...
               step=0.2; stagn=0
           if step < 1e-3:
               break
       if c5 < best_c: update
    final recompute best
    return best_h,best_c
```
If h_try same due projection (step too small) c_try5 not improve; eventually break.

```
def _logsum(vals, alpha):
   m=float(vals.max()); sh=alpha*(vals-m); mx=float(sh.max())
   if mx > 500:
       return m + (mx + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-mx))))) / alpha
   return m + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh)))) / alpha
```

`slsqp_polish`:
```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,deadline,max_rounds=2,alpha=500,top_m=10,maxiter=30):
   best_h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
   hh=best_h.copy()
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_eq_sum_fun,'jac':_eq_sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   for r in range(max_rounds):
      if time.time() > deadline-3: break
      c=compute_c_array(hh,n)
      if top_m >= c.size: top=np.arange(c.size)
      else: top=np.argsort(c)[-top_m:]
      # maybe sort not needed
      try:
         res = minimize(_obj_fixed_top, hh, args=(n, top, alpha), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-10})
         x = getattr(res,'x',None)
         if x is not None:
            hh2 = project_feasible(np.asarray(x,dtype=float),n)
            c2=compute_c_array(hh2,n); c52=float(c2.max())
            if c52 < best_c:
               best_c=c52; best_h=hh2
            hh = hh2
      except Exception:
         pass
   return best_h,best_c
```
Potential memory for bounds list n=1000 okay. If n > 800 skip in run.

```
def reflected_candidate(h,n):
   hr = np.asarray(h)[::-1].copy()
   hr = project_feasible(hr,n)
   return hr
def sym_candidate(h,n):
   hs = 0.5*(h + h[::-1])
   hs = project_feasible(hs,n)
   return hs
```

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c_array(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx
    return c
```
`np.correlate` with h float. If n Length? good.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(x, n):
    x = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    if x.size != n:
        x = x[:n] #? maybe not
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-12:
        return x
    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        y = np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        sy = float(y.sum())
        if sy > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if diff > 0:
        room = 1.0 - y
        tot = float(room.sum())
        if tot > 1e-15:
            y += diff * room / tot
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
            diff = target - float(y.sum())
    elif diff < 0:
        tot = float(y.sum())
        if tot > 1e-15:
            y += diff * (y / tot)
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
            diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if diff > 1e-13:
        for i in range(n):
            if diff <= 0.0: break
            add = min(diff, max(0.0, 1.0 - y[i]))
            y[i] += add
            diff -= add
    elif diff < -1e-13:
        for i in range(n):
            if diff >= 0.0: break
            sub = min(-diff, y[i])
            y[i] -= sub
            diff += sub
    return y
```
If x size mismatch maybe not. Good.

`smooth_top_weights` top-level:
```
def smooth_top_weights(vals, alpha):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    if vals.size == 0: return np.array([])
    m = float(vals.max())
    sh = alpha * (vals - m)
    mx = float(sh.max())
    w = np.exp(sh - mx)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
       w = np.ones_like(vals) / vals.size
    else: w /= s
    return w
```
Since sh <=0, exp safe. If alpha inf? no.

`top_gradients`:
```
def top_gradients(h,n,c,top_idx,alpha):
   top_idx = np.asarray(top_idx, dtype=int)
   g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
   if top_idx.size == 0: return g
   vals = c[top_idx]
   w = smooth_top_weights(vals, alpha)
   dx=2.0/n
   for j in range(top_idx.size):
      idx = int(top_idx[j]); weight = float(w[j])
      if weight <= 0: continue
      lag = idx - (n-1)
      if lag == 0: g += weight*(1-2*h)
      else:
         m = lag if lag>0 else -lag
         L=n-m
         if lag>0:
            g[:L] += weight*(1.0-h[m:])
            g[m:] += -weight*h[:L]
         else:
            g[:L] += -weight*h[m:]
            g[m:] += weight*(1.0-h[:L])
   return g*dx
```

For `add_candidate`, use `fracs = (1.0, 0.9, 0.6, 0.3)`? Large first. Use tuple. If d full makes one value maybe extreme. Good.
```
def add_candidate(cands, seen, h, src, dst, max_cands):
    if len(cands) >= max_cands or src == dst: return
    lim = min(float(h[src]), float(1.0 - h[dst]))
    if lim <= 1e-11: return
    lim = max(0.0, lim - 1e-12)
    # full first
    for fr in (1.0, 0.9, 0.6, 0.3):
        d = fr * lim
        if d <= 1e-10: continue
        key = (src, dst, int(round(d * 1000000.0)))
        if key in seen: continue
        seen.add(key)
        cands.append((src, dst, d))
        if len(cands) >= max_cands: return
```
If full lim causes exactly one endpoint; okay. `int(round(d*1e6))` could be 0 for d<0.5e-6; but d>1e-10. Many tiny distinct may collide; maybe if d tiny they are not useful. okay.

`generate_candidates`: Need `max_pairs` and `max_lags` ints. If max_pairs <=0, for pairs none. If max_lags <=0, no top; return random? We call with positive.

For lag0 high/low, use hi_count up to 3 maybe. Candidate count low->high.
```
def generate_candidates(h,c,n,max_lags,max_pairs,max_cands=300):
    cands=[]; seen=set()
    size=2*n-1
    if max_lags <= 0 or max_cands <=0: return cands
    if max_lags >= size:
       top_idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
    else:
       top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-max_lags:][::-1]
    for idx in top_idx:
       idx=int(idx)
       lag=idx-(n-1)
       if lag == 0:
          hc = min(3, n//2)
          if hc >0:
             hi = np.argsort(h)[-hc:][::-1]
             lo = np.argsort(h)[:hc]
             for li in lo:
                li=int(li); hi_list=hi
                if h[li] >= 0.5 - 1e-12: continue
                for hi_i in hi_list:
                   hi_i=int(hi_i)
                   if h[hi_i] <= 0.5 + 1e-12: continue
                   add_candidate(cands,seen,h,li,hi_i,max_cands)
                   if len(cands)>=max_cands: break
                if len(cands)>=max_cands: break
       else:
          m=lag if lag>0 else -lag
          L=n-m
          if L <=0: continue
          if lag>0:
             vals = h[:L] * (1.0 - h[m:])
          else:
             vals = h[m:] * (1.0 - h[:L])
          if vals.size ==0: continue
          if max_pairs <=0: continue
          if max_pairs >= vals.size:
             pp=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
          else:
             pp=np.argsort(vals)[-max_pairs:][::-1]
          for p in pp:
             p=int(p)
             if lag>0:
                add_candidate(cands,seen,h,p,p+m,max_cands)
             else:
                add_candidate(cands,seen,h,p+m,p,max_cands)
             if len(cands) < max_cands//2:
                if lag>0:
                   add_candidate(cands,seen,h,p+m,p,max_cands)
                else:
                   add_candidate(cands,seen,h,p,p+m,max_cands)
             if len(cands) >= max_cands: break
       if len(cands) >= max_cands: break
    return cands
```
Potential if `max_pairs//2` zero? max_pairs at least 1; len <150. okay.

`random_candidates`:
```
def random_candidates(h,n,rng,count,max_cands=80):
    cands=[]; seen=set()
    if n <2 or count <=0: return cands
    for _ in range(count):
       if len(cands) >= max_cands: break
       src=int(rng.randint(0,n)); dst=int(rng.randint(0,n))
       if src==dst: continue
       if rng.random() < 0.5:
          add_candidate(cands,seen,h,src,dst,max_cands)
       else:
          add_candidate(cands,seen,h,dst,src,max_cands)
    return cands
```

Let's write pair_search code:
```
def pair_search(h, n, deadline, rng, max_lags=8, max_pairs=4, max_cands=300, max_no_improve=60):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c_array(h, n)
    c5 = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = c5
    no_improve = 0; accepts = 0; perturb = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        accepted = False
        cands = generate_candidates(h, c, n, max_lags, max_pairs, max_cands)
        if len(cands) == 0:
            cands = random_candidates(h, n, rng, 50, max_cands)
        for src, dst, d in cands:
            if time.time() > deadline - 1.0: break
            if d <= 1e-11: continue
            if d > float(h[src]) + 1e-11 or d > 1.0 - float(h[dst]) + 1e-11: continue
            newc, new5 = fast_move_max(c, h, src, dst, d, n)
            if new5 < c5 - 1e-14:
                h[src] = max(0.0, min(1.0, h[src] - d))
                h[dst] = max(0.0, min(1.0, h[dst] + d))
                c = newc; c5 = new5; accepted=True; accepts += 1
                break
        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 5:
                cands2 = random_candidates(h, n, rng, 50, max_cands)
                for src, dst, d in cands2:
                    if time.time() > deadline - 1.0: break
                    if d <= 1e-11: continue
                    if d > float(h[src]) + 1e-11 or d > 1.0 - float(h[dst]) + 1e-11: continue
                    newc, new5 = fast_move_max(c, h, src, dst, d, n)
                    if new5 < c5 - 1e-14:
                        h[src] = max(0.0, min(1.0, h[src] - d))
                        h[dst] = max(0.0, min(1.0, h[dst] + d))
                        c = newc; c5 = new5; accepted=True; accepts += 1
                        break
        if accepted:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            if no_improve > max_no_improve:
                perturb += 1
                scale = 0.01 + 0.004 * float(min(perturb, 12))
                h2 = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n), n)
                c2 = compute_c_array(h2, n)
                c5_2 = float(c2.max())
                blend = project_feasible(0.5 * best_h + 0.5 * h2, n)
                c_b = compute_c_array(blend, n)
                c5_b = float(c_b.max())
                if c5_b < c5_2:
                    h2 = blend; c2 = c_b; c5_2 = c5_b
                h = h2; c = c2; c5 = c5_2
                no_improve = 0; accepts = 0
            elif no_improve > 3 * max_no_improve:
                break
        if c5 < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = c5; best_h = h.copy()
        if accepts > 0 and accepts % 50 == 0:
            c = compute_c_array(h, n)
            c5 = float(c.max())
            if c5 < best_c:
                best_c = c5; best_h = h.copy()
            elif c5 > best_c + 1e-7:
                h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
                c = compute_c_array(h, n)
                c5 = float(c.max())
                best_c = c5; best_h = h.copy()
                no_improve = 0; accepts = 0
    h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    c = compute_c_array(h, n)
    c5 = float(c.max())
    return h, c5
```
If recompute c5 exact lower than best, update. If exact worse than best by >1e-7, reset. Good.

```
def thue_morse_start(n, rng):
    out = np.zeros(n)
    for i in range(n):
        # parity of bit count
        out[i] = 1.0 if ((i.bit_count() & 1) == 0) else 0.0
    out = project_feasible(out,n)
    # add tiny noise to escape symmetry?
    out = project_feasible(out + rng.normal(0,0.01,n), n)
    return out
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def compute_c_array(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx


def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(compute_c_array(h, n)))


def project_feasible(x, n):
    x = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-12:
        return x

    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)

    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        y = np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        sy = float(y.sum())
        if sy > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    y = np.clip(x - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(y.sum())

    if diff > 0.0:
        room = 1.0 - y
        tot = float(room.sum())
        if tot > 1e-15:
            y += diff * room / tot
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
            diff = target - float(y.sum())
    elif diff < 0.0:
        tot = float(y.sum())
        if tot > 1e-15:
            y += diff * (y / tot)
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
            diff = target - float(y.sum())

    if diff > 1e-13:
        for i in range(n):
            if diff <= 0.0:
                break
            add = min(diff, max(0.0, 1.0 - y[i]))
            if add > 0.0:
                y[i] += add
                diff -= add
    elif diff < -1e-13:
        for i in range(n):
            if diff >= 0.0:
                break
            sub = min(-diff, y[i])
            if sub > 0.0:
                y[i] -= sub
                diff += sub

    return y


def sym_softer(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    x = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(x, n)


def thue_morse_start(n, rng):
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        out[i] = 1.0 if ((i.bit_count() & 1) == 0) else 0.0
    out = project_feasible(out, n)
    out = project_feasible(out + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n), n)
    return out


def interpolate_h(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    h_old = np.asarray(h_old, dtype=float)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return h_old.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old)


def smooth_top_weights(vals, alpha):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    if vals.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    m = float(vals.max())
    shifted = alpha * (vals - m)
    mx = float(shifted.max())
    w = np.exp(shifted - mx)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones_like(vals) / float(vals.size)
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def logsum_exp_values(vals, alpha):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    m = float(vals.max())
    shifted = alpha * (vals - m)
    mx = float(shifted.max())
    if mx > 500.0:
        return m + (mx + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(shifted - mx))))) / alpha
    return m + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(shifted)))) / alpha


def top_gradients(h, n, c, top_idx, alpha):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    top_idx = np.asarray(top_idx, dtype=int)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if top_idx.size == 0:
        return g

    vals = c[top_idx]
    w = smooth_top_weights(vals, alpha)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)

    for j in range(top_idx.size):
        idx = int(top_idx[j])
        weight = float(w[j])
        if weight <= 0.0:
            continue
        lag = idx - (n - 1)
        if lag == 0:
            g += weight * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        else:
            m = lag if lag > 0 else -lag
            L = n - m
            if lag > 0:
                g[:L] += weight * (1.0 - h[m:])
                g[m:] += -weight * h[:L]
            else:
                g[:L] += -weight * h[m:]
                g[m:] += weight * (1.0 - h[:L])

    return g * dx


def gradient_search(h, n, deadline, rng, top_m=8, alpha=300.0):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c_array(h, n)
    c5 = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5

    step = 0.3
    stagnation = 0

    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        c = compute_c_array(h, n)
        c5 = float(c.max())
        if c5 < best_c:
            best_c = c5
            best_h = h.copy()

        if top_m >= c.size:
            top_idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        else:
            top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-top_m:][::-1]

        g = top_gradients(h, n, c, top_idx, alpha)
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if norm < 1e-12:
            break

        direction = -g / norm
        accepted = False

        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125):
            h_try = project_feasible(h + step * mult * direction, n)
            c_try = compute_c_array(h_try, n)
            c5_try = float(c_try.max())
            if c5_try < c5 - 1e-13:
                h = h_try
                c = c_try
                c5 = c5_try
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            stagnation = 0
            step = min(1.0, step * 1.08)
        else:
            stagnation += 1
            step *= 0.8
            if stagnation > 40:
                h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n), n)
                c = compute_c_array(h, n)
                c5 = float(c.max())
                step = 0.2
                stagnation = 0
            if step < 1e-3:
                break

        if c5 < best_c:
            best_c = c5
            best_h = h.copy()

    h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    c5 = compute_c5(h, n)
    return h, c5


def add_candidate(cands, seen, h, src, dst, max_cands):
    if len(cands) >= max_cands or src == dst:
        return
    lim = min(float(h[src]), float(1.0 - h[dst]))
    if lim <= 1e-11:
        return
    lim = max(0.0, lim - 1e-12)

    for fr in (1.0, 0.9, 0.6, 0.3):
        d = fr * lim
        if d <= 1e-10:
            continue
        key = (src, dst, int(round(d * 1000000.0)))
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        cands.append((src, dst, d))
        if len(cands) >= max_cands:
            return


def generate_candidates(h, c, n, max_lags, max_pairs, max_cands=300):
    cands = []
    seen = set()
    if max_lags <= 0 or max_cands <= 0:
        return cands

    size = 2 * n - 1
    if max_lags >= size:
        top_idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
    else:
        top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-max_lags:][::-1]

    for idx in top_idx:
        idx = int(idx)
        lag = idx - (n - 1)

        if lag == 0:
            hc = min(3, n // 2)
            if hc > 0:
                hi = np.argsort(h)[-hc:][::-1]
                lo = np.argsort(h)[:hc]
                for li in lo:
                    li = int(li)
                    if h[li] >= 0.5 - 1e-12:
                        continue
                    for hi_i in hi:
                        hi_i = int(hi_i)
                        if h[hi_i] <= 0.5 + 1e-12:
                            continue
                        add_candidate(cands, seen, h, li, hi_i, max_cands)
                        if len(cands) >= max_cands:
                            break
                    if len(cands) >= max_cands:
                        break
        else:
            m = lag if lag > 0 else -lag
            L = n - m
            if L <= 0 or max_pairs <= 0:
                continue

            if lag > 0:
                vals = h[:L] * (1.0 - h[m:])
            else:
                vals = h[m:] * (1.0 - h[:L])

            if vals.size == 0:
                continue

            if max_pairs >= vals.size:
                pp = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
            else:
                pp = np.argsort(vals)[-max_pairs:][::-1]

            for p in pp:
                p = int(p)
                if lag > 0:
                    add_candidate(cands, seen, h, p, p + m, max_cands)
                else:
                    add_candidate(cands, seen, h, p + m, p, max_cands)

                if len(cands) < max_cands // 2:
                    if lag > 0:
                        add_candidate(cands, seen, h, p + m, p, max_cands)
                    else:
                        add_candidate(cands, seen, h, p, p + m, max_cands)

                if len(cands) >= max_cands:
                    break

        if len(cands) >= max_cands:
            break

    return cands


def random_candidates(h, n, rng, count, max_cands=80):
    cands = []
    seen = set()
    if n < 2 or count <= 0:
        return cands

    for _ in range(count):
        if len(cands) >= max_cands:
            break
        src = int(rng.randint(0, n))
        dst = int(rng.randint(0, n))
        if src == dst:
            continue
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            add_candidate(cands, seen, h, src, dst, max_cands)
        else:
            add_candidate(cands, seen, h, dst, src, max_cands)

    return cands


def fast_move_max(c, h, src, dst, d, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    cn = c.copy()
    idx0 = n - 1

    hs = float(h[src])
    hd = float(h[dst])

    ns = hs - d
    cn[idx0] += (ns * (1.0 - ns) - hs * (1.0 - hs)) * dx
    nd = hd + d
    cn[idx0] += (nd * (1.0 - nd) - hd * (1.0 - hd)) * dx

    absd = src - dst
    if absd < 0:
        absd = -absd

    nn = n
    for m in range(1, nn):
        dp = 0.0
        dn = 0.0

        b = src + m
        if b < nn:
            da = -d
            db = d if (b == dst) else 0.0
            hb = h[b]
            dp += da * (1.0 - hb) - hs * db - da * db
            dn += db * (1.0 - hs) - hb * da - db * da

        if not (src > dst and m == absd):
            a = src - m
            if a >= 0:
                da = d if (a == dst) else 0.0
                db = -d
                ha = h[a]
                dp += da * (1.0 - hs) - ha * db - da * db
                dn += db * (1.0 - ha) - hs * da - db * da

        b = dst + m
        if b < nn:
            da = d
            db = -d if (b == src) else 0.0
            hb = h[b]
            dp += da * (1.0 - hb) - hd * db - da * db
            dn += db * (1.0 - hd) - hb * da - db * da

        if not (src < dst and m == absd):
            a = dst - m
            if a >= 0:
                da = -d if (a == src) else 0.0
                db = d
                ha = h[a]
                dp += da * (1.0 - hd) - ha * db - da * db
                dn += db * (1.0 - ha) - hd * da - db * da

        if dp != 0.0:
            cn[idx0 + m] += dp * dx
        if dn != 0.0:
            cn[idx0 - m] += dn * dx

    return cn, float(np.max(cn))


def pair_search(h, n, deadline, rng,
                max_lags=8, max_pairs=4, max_cands=300,
                max_no_improve=60):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c_array(h, n)
    c5 = float(c.max())

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5

    no_improve = 0
    accepts = 0
    perturb = 0

    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        accepted = False

        cands = generate_candidates(h, c, n, max_lags, max_pairs, max_cands)
        if len(cands) == 0:
            cands = random_candidates(h, n, rng, 50, max_cands)

        for src, dst, d in cands:
            if time.time() > deadline - 1.0:
                break
            if d <= 1e-11:
                continue
            if d > float(h[src]) + 1e-11 or d > 1.0 - float(h[dst]) + 1e-11:
                continue

            newc, new5 = fast_move_max(c, h, src, dst, d, n)
            if new5 < c5 - 1e-14:
                h[src] = max(0.0, min(1.0, h[src] - d))
                h[dst] = max(0.0, min(1.0, h[dst] + d))
                c = newc
                c5 = new5
                accepted = True
                accepts += 1
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 5:
                cands2 = random_candidates(h, n, rng, 50, max_cands)
                for src, dst, d in cands2:
                    if time.time() > deadline - 1.0:
                        break
                    if d <= 1e-11:
                        continue
                    if d > float(h[src]) + 1e-11 or d > 1.0 - float(h[dst]) + 1e-11:
                        continue

                    newc, new5 = fast_move_max(c, h, src, dst, d, n)
                    if new5 < c5 - 1e-14:
                        h[src] = max(0.0, min(1.0, h[src] - d))
                        h[dst] = max(0.0, min(1.0, h[dst] + d))
                        c = newc
                        c5 = new5
                        accepted = True
                        accepts += 1
                        break

        if accepted:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            if no_improve > max_no_improve:
                perturb += 1
                scale = 0.01 + 0.004 * float(min(perturb, 12))
                h2 = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n), n)
                c2 = compute_c_array(h2, n)
                c5_2 = float(c2.max())

                blend = project_feasible(0.5 * best_h + 0.5 * h2, n)
                cb = compute_c_array(blend, n)
                c5_b = float(cb.max())
                if c5_b < c5_2:
                    h2 = blend
                    c2 = cb
                    c5_2 = c5_b

                h = h2
                c = c2
                c5 = c5_2
                no_improve = 0
                accepts = 0
            elif no_improve > 3 * max_no_improve:
                break

        if c5 < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = c5
            best_h = h.copy()

        if accepts > 0 and accepts % 50 == 0:
            c = compute_c_array(h, n)
            c5 = float(c.max())
            if c5 < best_c:
                best_c = c5
                best_h = h.copy()
            elif c5 > best_c + 1e-7:
                h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
                c = compute_c_array(h, n)
                c5 = float(c.max())
                best_c = c5
                best_h = h.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                accepts = 0

    h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    c5 = compute_c5(h, n)
    return h, c5


def _eq_sum_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))


def _eq_sum_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def _obj_fixed_top(x, n, top_idx, alpha):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full') * dx
    return logsum_exp_values(c[top_idx], alpha)


def _jac_fixed_top(x, n, top_idx, alpha):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    c = compute_c_array(x, n)
    return top_gradients(x, n, c, top_idx, alpha)


def slsqp_polish(h, n, deadline, max_rounds=1, alpha=500.0, top_m=10, maxiter=25):
    best_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    hh = best_h.copy()

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _eq_sum_fun, 'jac': _eq_sum_jac, 'args': (n,)}]

    for _ in range(max_rounds):
        if time.time() > deadline - 3.0:
            break
        c = compute_c_array(hh, n)
        if top_m >= c.size:
            top = np.arange(c.size)
        else:
            top = np.argsort(c)[-top_m:]
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _obj_fixed_top,
                hh,
                args=(n, top, alpha),
                method='SLSQP',
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-10}
            )
            x = getattr(res, 'x', None)
            if x is not None:
                hh2 = project_feasible(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), n)
                c2 = compute_c_array(hh2, n)
                c52 = float(c2.max())
                if c52 < best_c:
                    best_c = c52
                    best_h = hh2
                hh = hh2
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float('inf')

    try:
        h_init = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n_init = len(h_init)
        if n_init < 8:
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        n_init = 500
        h_init = thue_morse_start(n_init, rng)

    h_init = project_feasible(h_init, n_init)
    c_init = compute_c_array(h_init, n_init)
    c5_init = float(c_init.max())
    best_h = h_init
    best_c = c5_init

    # Main intensification at the parent resolution.
    if time.time() < deadline - 60.0:
        alloc = min(420.0, 0.45 * (deadline - time.time()))
        end = min(deadline - 2.0, time.time() + alloc)
        if end - time.time() > 20.0:
            try:
                hp, cp = pair_search(best_h, n_init, end, rng,
                                     max_lags=8, max_pairs=4,
                                     max_cands=350, max_no_improve=70)
                if cp < best_c:
                    best_c = cp
                    best_h = hp
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Cheap symmetrization candidate.
    if time.time() < deadline - 5.0 and best_h is not None:
        n_b = len(best_h)
        try:
            hs = sym_softer(best_h, n_b)
            cs = compute_c_array(hs, n_b)
            c5s = float(cs.max())
            if c5s < best_c:
                best_h = hs
                best_c = c5s
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Global/vector gradient polishing.
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
        n_b = len(best_h)
        if n_b <= 900:
            alloc = min(100.0, 0.20 * (deadline - time.time()))
            end = min(deadline - 2.0, time.time() + alloc)
            if end - time.time() > 20.0:
                try:
                    hp, cp = gradient_search(best_h, n_b, end, rng,
                                             top_m=8, alpha=300.0)
                    if cp < best_c:
                        best_c = cp
                        best_h = hp
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # SLSQP polishing at modest resolution.
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 50.0:
        n_b = len(best_h)
        if n_b <= 620:
            alloc = min(45.0, 0.25 * (deadline - time.time()))
            end = min(deadline - 2.0, time.time() + alloc)
            if end - time.time() > 20.0:
                try:
                    hp, cp = slsqp_polish(best_h, n_b, end,
                                          max_rounds=1, alpha=500.0,
                                          top_m=10, maxiter=25)
                    if cp < best_c:
                        best_c = cp
                        best_h = hp
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Try a few higher resolutions, starting from the current best.
    if best_h is not None:
        base = len(best_h)
        n_candidates = []
        for ntry in (base + 150, 650, 850, 1024, base + 300):
            if ntry <= base:
                continue
            if ntry % 2 != 0:
                ntry += 1
            if ntry > 1400:
                continue
            if ntry not in n_candidates:
                n_candidates.append(ntry)

        for ntry in n_candidates:
            remaining = deadline - time.time()
            if remaining < 90.0:
                break

            try:
                h_try = interpolate_h(best_h, base, ntry)
                h_try = project_feasible(h_try, ntry)
            except Exception:
                break

            c_try = compute_c_array(h_try, ntry)
            c5_try = float(c_try.max())
            if c5_try < best_c:
                best_c = c5_try
                best_h = h_try

            alloc = min(90.0, 0.30 * (deadline - time.time()))
            if alloc < 30.0:
                break
            end = min(deadline - 2.0, time.time() + alloc)
            if end - time.time() < 20.0:
                break

            try:
                hp, cp = pair_search(h_try, ntry, end, rng,
                                     max_lags=7, max_pairs=3,
                                     max_cands=250, max_no_improve=50)
                if cp < best_c:
                    best_c = cp
                    best_h = hp
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final global polish.
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 25.0:
        n_b = len(best_h)
        end = deadline - 1.0
        if end - time.time() > 15.0:
            try:
                hp, cp = pair_search(best_h, n_b, end, rng,
                                     max_lags=10, max_pairs=4,
                                     max_cands=400, max_no_improve=90)
                if cp < best_c:
                    best_c = cp
                    best_h = hp
            except Exception:
                pass

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = project_feasible(np.full(500, 0.5, dtype=float), 500)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, 500)

    n_final = len(best_h)
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n_final)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n_final)

    return best_h, float(best_c), n_final
```
